### Post #37: 2024-10-21 21:13 — `$ELTP` `[Dd]`

**My DD on $ELTP**

This is the first DD I’ve ever written so you have my apologies in advance. Please feel free to ask any questions)

Please keep in mind that I’ve only been trading for 4-5 months. I appreciate all comments & further DD

First I’d like to say that I am a person in recovery & that is why I started looking into this company. But hey, you’re not here for my story you’re here to make money so let’s get down to it. 

I first noticed $ELTP in June/July ‘24.  They are a small cap Pharmaceutical Company based out of NJ. They specialize in producing generic medications. What I noticed is that they also make meds (amongst other types of meds) to help recovering alcoholics & addicts, but try to stay away from addictive opioids.  It appears they release a new med of different sorts every 8 weeks or so. 

Over the past 3 months the SP has risen an astronomical 271%, and close to 500% over 1 year! This is not a short squeeze. From what I can see on Fintel, the short percentage is a mere 0.25% of the float. How amazing is that for natural growth?!?!

Over the summer, ELTP recruited some rockstars in the Pharmaceutical Sales space to help lead the sales team & it seems to be working. 

Last week they were approved to distribute generic Adderall in Israel which opens up a global market possibility, and now they are in talks for distribution in some European Countries as well. 

The company has already been approved by the FDA to produce generic Vyvanse of which there is currently a shortage. The DEA is supposed to inspect the manufacturing facility in November. ELTP (Elite Pharmaceuticals) has already produced this product & is holding it in stable storage until the facility is approved. They are ready to start shipping this product!

Elite won a court case to produce generic OxyContin as well & they have the exclusive rights to produce the generic version for a limited time (I forget exactly how long, but I think it’s 6 months). The case has been fully settled including appeals. 

The target price for the 2 years was just upgraded to $4-5/share. The RSI is 84 & signals that the stock is overbought, however the SP keeps rising. At this time stockinvest.us gives a Strong Buy rating of 8.56. Since the summer they have downgraded the risk factor from Highest to High. 

The market cap is 624.938M with a P/E ratio of 31.91. Stockinvest.us does not have a ceiling for this stock although as recently as this morning they said the “fan theory” was $0.65. Well guess what, they blew that away today & closed at $0.705!

There is very strong insider support per last 100 transactions:
Buy: 5, 450, 759, 530, 344 shares
Sell: 3, 265, 299, 550, 344 shares

Giving a positive insider rate of 72.42

This stock is considered high risk, but also high reward. Today $ELTP closed +20.51% with a SP of $0.705

Last Friday I exited a YOLO & some of those funds to buy 4,000 shares. I bout more today bringing my share count to 5,034. 

I hope this helps anyone who may be interested in purchasing this stock. Again, please feel free to ask questions & I will answer them as best I can. I also encourage more DD than I have been able to provide. 

Edit: I use Fidelity. This stock may not be available on your Brokerage yet

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/i1v/my-dd-on-dollareltp*

### Post #48: 2024-10-24 20:10 — `$TSLA, $MNPR` `[Loss]` `[Loss]`

**Emotional Trading**

Today I made a huge mistake that I’d like to share so that it may possibly help others. 

Today I saw the insane gains happening live with $MNPR. I bought in after the 4th halt & was up $450 within a few seconds! It halted again & bought in for a few grand which is good size of my portfolio. I was up even more, & then the stock CRASHED. I was down 33% or $900 and I sold due to emotional trading. 

If I held the stock I would’ve made $1500-$2K instead of taking the $900 L. 

I didn’t follow the plan to hold & that was my emotional mistake. 

Always have a plan before you jump in. I also didn’t set my Stop Loss bc of all the halting & I was sure the stock would continue its meteoric daily rise. 

Guess what, the stock closed $13.66 above where I sold. Instead of making $1500-$2K today, I ended up down $515. This could’ve been my best day yet in trading. 

How could this be a positive post with such a bad swing? Well, I’m an experienced based learner. Today I learned a valuable lesson to follow my plan. 

I told other folks in $TSLA what my gut feeling this morning. about holding, not selling,  & those folks ended up making a lot more money than they planned on. 

Take the emotion out of trading, stick to your game plan, and you’ll have a better chance of success. 

I hope you all had a beautiful Green Day!



*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/jeq/emotional-trading*

### Post #217: 2024-11-20 02:05 — `$MSTR`

**Trading without emotion**

I was asked to write a post on removing emotion from trading, the downfalls that I’ve struggled with, & removing emotion has helped me as a trader. 

Firstly we should be honest, we are investing our hard earned money & that makes it personal. When we are personally invested in anything we as humans typically involve emotion. 

I want to be the most successful trader I can possibly be. I want to grow my account fast & have a great tolerance for high risk/high reward. These 2 things led me to trade based on emotion/greed. I wanted to make all the gains on all the trades all the time. Guess what folks, that simply is not going to happen. That’s a fact of trading. 

My greed allowed me to pass up modest gains in hopes of huge gains, only to see those modest gains turn into losses. 

Once I had a trading plan in place it allowed me to remove emotion from the trade because the exit strategy was no longer up to me, it was up to the trading plan. 

My trading plan: Red days are holding or buying, Green days are for holding or taking profit. I don’t trade puts yet. When trading options I exit with a 20-30% profit. I only break this rule when trading options on $MSTR because that is a special, volatile stock, with potential for astronomical gains. Never blindly follow, always do some DD on your own. Take advice from those you’ve gotten to know are trustworthy. Always trade with conviction. 

If I follow my plan I know I’m giving it my all & I can not be upset with the outcome. We are not going to be profitable with every trade, although that is obviously the goal. I do not consider my losses as loss, but rather  as fee paid to become a more profitable trader in the future. As long as I learn from my mistakes then they will be helpful in the future. 

I hope this helps. If anyone has any questions I am happy to answer. 

I wish you all good fortune!


*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/lFX/trading-without-emotion*

---

### Post #268: 2024-11-25 02:49

**Over 1500 followers**

Thank you to the 1500+ AfterHour users that choose to follow me on my adventures in the “Pond”!

Who’s ready for a little back story behind my username? Well here it is:

3 months ago when I joined AfterHour I barely knew what a Call Option was. The only thing I was certain of was that I had gone through a major life change & wanted to try my hand at trading stocks. I had no experience & no knowledge. I was the “New Fish” attempting to swim in the “Big Pond” called the Stock Market. Ergo, NewFishBigPond

The AfterHour community was so welcoming to me & willing teach a guy that asked ALL of the “dumb” questions. They didn’t mind answering my questions because everyone starts somewhere. 

Through the knowledge I’ve gained here in the past 3 months, I was able to grow my account by 150%! Wall Street traders would kill for a return like that, but the NewFishBigPond was able to achieve it. 

I’m posting this hoping that I can inspire just one person. With determination & dedication it is possible to achieve your goals, beat the statistics, & have a great deal of fun while doing it!

I love trading stocks! I love the gains & believe it or not I actually like the losses too. Why would I like the losses?? That’s crazy, right? Not to me. I learn from each loss & view them as a fee paid to become a more profitable trader in the future. 

Please, never hesitate to reach out with questions! If I can’t answer them I can usually loop in the right person to get your answer. We have such a collective knowledge base here at our fingertips & if we take advantage of the crowdsourced knowledge then we have an advantage over most folks. 

To the OGs who helped me learn so much in such a short amount of time, I thank you dearly from the bottom of my heart. You have all played such an important role in my journey thus far, & I appreciate you more than you know. Y’all know who you are. 

@SirJack, as I have said so many times & will continue to say, THANK YOU for creating such an amazing space for us to come together to learn, grow, make money, & have fun while we’re doing it. 

AfterHour is a very special community. 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/mtP/over-1500-followers*

### Post #278: 2024-11-26 00:54 — `$MSTR`

**What happened to $MSTR today & what I’ve dug up about it**

Well, if you hold $MSTR you probably didn’t have a great day unless you had Puts or short shares 😂 

We’ve lost a lot of our profits recently. Here’s what I’ve been able to dig up between trusted sources & Fintel.io:

BTC was, & still is, being shorted…. up to 60% in some markets

Friday had lowest FINRA Short Share Volume over the past 2 weeks which is as far back as I can see. Today had the heaviest FINRA Short Share Volume in a week & the 3rd heaviest over 2 weeks. Here are the numbers:

Friday Short Share Volume: 34.81
Monday (today) Short Share Volume: 46.33

Although today was the 3rd heaviest Short Share Volume in 2 weeks, it was much worse because BTC was being shorted so heavily AT THE SAME TIME! This makes today the heaviest Short $MSTR has experienced in the past 2 weeks (again, this is as far back as I can see)

$MSTR is a volatile stock. In fact, per Michael Saylor, volatility is there purposefully. This stock is not for the average trader. This stock is like riding a roller coaster & if you get off at the wrong time it can do some serious damage to your account. 

If you’re trading Options, especially on $MSTR, please ensure you know how to trade Options…very well. This could decimate an account if it doesn’t go your way and/or you don’t know what your doing with Options trading. 

I have fielded many questions throughout the day, & forced thoughts out of my own head. People are scared of losing their money & that’s a fair feeling to have. If this sounds like you, then you may want to get off this ride before it gets bumpier, and trust me it will get bumpier. The Short Sellers will not simply walk away after a few days. 

However, Michael Saylor will not stop fighting for his company, his shareholders, or his vision. 

Please do whatever you feel is best for your portfolio. 

As for me, I like the stock. I’m not selling!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/mFg/what-happened-to-dollarmstr-to*

Well, yesterday KULR tweeted that another partnership will be announced soon & to me it looks like this partnership may possibly be with $TSLA for their robots.

### Post #320: 2024-11-29 21:15

**2000 followers…..WOW!**

I am beyond humbled that 2000 of my fellow AfterHour users have chosen to follow me! I am truly amazed. 

When I joined AfterHour 3 months ago (with basically zero knowledge of the Stock Market)  I never thought I would have 2000 people following me I wouldn’t have believed it. 

2000 people that think I have an interesting perspective, hope to share in the knowledge that I’ve gained here, or want to see what I’m buying & selling. I am in a bit of disbelief. At the same time, I’m very happy that y’all find what I have to say or trade interesting.

### Post #329: 2024-12-01 00:15 — `$GME`

**Another reason I’m bullish on $GME …. Oh & Trump says he’s going to make naked shorting illegal as well!**

After looking at Fintel.io it appears the shorts have been reducing their short positions which is currently just above 27%. 

I found this on Reddit & thought I’d share it here:

BREAKING: Charles Schwab Investment Management Inc. Increases GME Holdings by 22.6% – What’s Really Going On with all these Institutional Buy-Ins? 🚨

💎 *Schwab just made a BIG move!* Charles Schwab Investment Management Inc. raised its stake in **GameStop Corp. (NYSE:GME)** by **22.6%** in Q3, adding **646,488 shares** to bring their total holdings to **3,502,910 shares**, worth **$80.3M**. That’s no small change for a “we just like the stock” situation. But… let’s take a step back and ask: **Why?**

---

## **Key Highlights:**
- Schwab now owns **0.82% of GameStop’s outstanding shares**, as of their latest SEC filing.
- Other major institutional moves:
  - **Van ECK Associates Corp:** Up 8.1%, holding **1.83M shares** ($41.9M).
  - **Renaissance Technologies LLC:** Boosted holdings by **34%**, holding **1.35M shares** ($33.3M).
  - **Swiss National Bank:** Raised their stake by **46%**, with **790,528 shares** ($18.1M).
  - Institutional ownership now stands at **29.21% of GME stock**.

---

## **Thinking Outside the Box:**
Sure, on the surface, institutional buying can be seen as a bullish sign, showing confidence in the company’s fundamentals. But **what if** there’s another angle? Institutions like Schwab don’t just *buy and hold*—they’re also in the business of **securities lending**. 

### Could Schwab be buying to loan shares out?
1. **Lending Shares = Big Money**: Institutions can earn significant fees by loaning out shares to short sellers. Could Schwab be positioning itself to profit off retail sentiment by increasing the available float for shorts?
2. **Facilitating Market Liquidity**: It’s no secret that short sellers rely on borrowed shares to keep the pressure on. By holding more GME, Schwab may be enabling shorting activities on its platform.
3. **Counterpoint**: It’s also possible that Schwab is simply diversifying or aligning with a long-term strategy—institutions have access to information and models we don’t.

---

### **What Does This Mean for Us?**
We can’t ignore the possibility that Schwab’s move could work *against* retail investors if those shares are loaned out. But this doesn’t change the fact that **institutional ownership is climbing**—a sign that GME remains a valuable (and closely watched) asset.

At the same time, let's remain vigilant and skeptical. Institutions don’t always align with retail interests, but that’s exactly why **we like the stock.**

---

🎉 **TL;DR:** Charles Schwab boosted its GME holdings by 22.6% to $80.3M. Is this a bullish signal, or are these shares being used to feed short sellers? Either way, institutional interest in GME is rising—something the community should watch closely.

---
**Source:** [MarketBeat](https://www.marketbeat.com/instant-alerts/charles-schwab-investment-management-inc-raises-position-in-gamestop-corp-nysegme-2024-11-28/)  

What do you think? Is Schwab bullish, or are they playing both sides?

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/nm1/another-reason-im-bullish-on-d*

### Post #351: 2024-12-04 02:23

**How do I find out the short information of a stock?**

I use Fintel.io a lot to get the short interest information. When you search “RXRX SHORT” it will bring you to that number in the section at the top of the screen. 

That is misleading bc it’s only updated every 2 weeks. You need to scroll down to see the short shares sold through the day. If you scroll down further you can see the short shares volume ratio for the past 2 weeks. 

Using that information is how I figure out how badly a stock is being shorted.

Bookmark this post so you can easily access it when you need it

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/nYt/how-do-i-find-out-the-short-in*

### Post #651: 2025-01-22 23:19

**Why do I “leave money on the table” ?**

Some people have asked why I “sell early” or “leave money on the table”? 

Answer: to realize profit

I have a trading plan that I adhere to 90% of the time. That trading plan tells me to take profits at 20-30% & risk the same on Options. Many times I’ll get out of a trade as soon as it hits my 20% target & this is why….greed. 

In the past my greed for profit has seen a +40% turn into a -20%. This means I lost money on a trade that should’ve been profitable. Although it wasn’t a total loss because I did learn a valuable lesson to take profits. It’s not “leaving money on the table” if you’re putting realized profit into your account.

I have a higher risk tolerance than most for my account size so keep that in mind when deciding on your profit/loss target percentages 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/zci/why-do-i-leave-money-on-the-ta*

**Something to remember….**

If your port opens & closes red tomorrow it’s nothing to worry about right now. Stocks go up, stocks down, stocks go up.  The red you might see is realized if you hit the sell button. Until then it’s just a loss on paper. 

On red days I like to see what’s at a discount that I’d like to own & make some buys if I can. 

I hope everyone has a profitable week

### Post #677: 2025-01-29 23:20

**Are you mentally prepared for your trading day?**

I’ve been going through some things in my personal life lately. I’ve noticed that I haven’t been trading as well as I should be. I haven’t been sticking to my trading plan & taking riskier trades than I normally do. 

If you’re not mentally prepared to trade on a given day then my advice is to just “sit on your hands”.  This is your investment, your money, so let’s not squander it away on days we’re not mentally ready. 

Remember, it’s ok to watch the market to see how your positions are holding up & sitting cash is always an option. 

Wishing everyone good fortune!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/Ayz/are-you-mentally-prepared-for-*

### Post #678: 2025-01-30 00:00 — `$NNE`

**I had limit order set for $NNE tonight & it went through on a dip**

I saw that Trump put out a Truth that the US is going to “unleash” the energy sector & deregulate it possibly. I was able to add 90 shares for a total of 100 shares. 

Why did I want 100 shares? This way I can sell a covered call if I want to make a little money from the premium. As long as I do a call at a strike above my cost basis then I’ll make money from the premium & profit if the shares get exercised. If the shares aren’t exercised then I get to sell another CC the following week

### Post #721: 2025-02-15 12:13

**9500 followers—Thank you!**

Somehow over 9500 AfterHour users found their way to my page, enjoyed the content I post, & decided to hit the follow button 🤯 

Thank you all for following me…it really means a lot to me. I hope you’ve enjoyed watching my trading journey. I post my trades, & hopefully we are all making together. Some weeks I post & trade more than others. It all depends on what I see that’s right for my portfolio at the time.

### Post #729: 2025-02-23 12:04

**Do you have a trading plan?**

I did a poll about a week ago asking this very same question. I was shocked to find out that 88% of ppl that answered the poll do NOT have a trading plan. 

A trading plan is important. Think of it as your personalized set of rules for how you trade. Are you risky or conservative? Do you trade options? When do you take profits? Do you sell covered calls?

Only you can develop the right trading plan for yourself   

Here’s very simple example:

On options trades is typically look for 20-30%. Why? Because I’ve been burned by my own greed always wanting more, but Mr. Market doesn’t always like that, so I decided that 20-30% is good for me. 

I also like to sell covered calls as part of my plan. I always set the strike price at a profit I’d be happy with. This way I can collect premiums & if the stock goes down I will still be profitable or at breakeven. I sell the calls on Fridays so that Theta can work for me over the weekend. 

Find your niche: meaning find a strategy that resonates with you & follow it to perfection. I‘be playin off of market fear for the past month with this particular strategy I’ve profited 4/4 times. 

When do you cut your losses? I cut losses at 20%. I’ve decided this is the risk percentage I’m comfortable with. 

Keep in mind this is a basic version of my trading plan. I have found that following my trading plan helps me limit losses. When I don’t follow this plan I have found that I am less profitable. 

Soooooo, what I’m saying is this, if you don’t have a trading plan you should probably develop one. You try it out with a paper trading account if you’d like to see if your plan works for you or if you need to tweak it a bit. At the same time if you’re making money hand over fist then keep doing what you’re doing…. You’ve figured it out, but that’s not the case for most folks. 

Feel free to bookmark this post for easy reference. 

I wish you all good fortune 🍀 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/E1f/do-you-have-a-trading-plan*

**It’s official & OMFG, I just can’t believe this**

I am beyond amazed that I reached the 10,000 follower milestone! A special thank you to each & everyone of you that follows me. I am beside myself right now. 

I joined AfterHour 5 months ago with zero knowledge of the stock market or how to trade. The only thing I knew is that I was hungry for knowledge. I craved gaining knowledge & spent so many hours scouring posts for insights, asking a bunch of “dumb” questions, paying attention to ppl that had a trading style I was interested in, & absorbed all the information I could. 

Here I am 5 months later & I’m able to confidently answer questions from newer users, I’ve completed a $10-25K challenge (in about 3 months), & I honestly don’t how this was possible. I have no background in finance, & I didn’t go to college. I guess what I’m trying to say is if you apply yourself you can reach goals you never even dreamed of. 

 @SirJack I cannot thank you enough for creating this amazing platform called AfterHour. I’ve said it many times & I’ll say it again, this is such a special community. We are stronger together. 

To the newer users: please never hesitate to reach out to me with questions. I’m more than happy to help you find the knowledge you’re looking for. If I don’t have the answer I can loop in the right person 99% of the time. I would also like to remind everyone that there are no “dumb” questions on AfterHour. So, if you’ve gained knowledge from me or if I’ve been able to help you in anyway I’m going to ask you what was asked of me when I started learning: when it’s your turn just pay it forward & teach others. 

This community celebrates wins & consoles losses. We are all here for the same reason which is to better ourselves & our families. We are stronger together. 

To the OGs that helped me along the way I sincerely appreciate all that you’ve helped me learn. 

I really don’t know what else to say bc this is a bit surreal right now. I guess what I’m saying is THANK YOU to everyone for being here on AfterHour.

### Post #830: 2025-04-03 00:27

**Strap in everyone**

Things could be a bit more bumpy for a little longer. We’re still unsure of what to expect with the tariffs… how will other Nations react to this? How will the US react to the reaction of the other Nations? Will the tariffs increase, decrease, or disappear? Nobody is certain. 

One thing that is certain is that a Market Maker’s job is to separate you from you from your money. We’re retail  traders; they needs us as their liquidity. This is why it’s so important to think about market psychology. 

I often think of what “we”/retail expects the market will do. Then I do the opposite in an attempt to gain an upper hand or get in early  using the market psychology against the Market Makers. 

What “tricks” do you use to gain an advantage or get in early on a trade?

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/JEs/strap-in-everyone*

### Post #835: 2025-04-03 10:41

**How will you react to the downturn?**

Ok, so we have the biggest tariff implementation in ~100 years that absolutely crushed the market after yesterday’s close. How will you react to this? It’s important to think about this so you don’t make a rash decision. 

The fact is that during economic downturns or recessions the traders who are most rewarded are those that hold & add to their positions. Take advantage of the deals available to you. Increase your position in your favorite holdings or add a new one. 

Remember: unrealized losses are temporary, but the sell button is permanent 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/JGa/how-will-you-react-to-the-down*

### Post #861: 2025-04-19 01:12 `[Discuss]`

**Do you have a trading plan?**

A trading plan is important. Think of it as your personalized set of rules for how you trade. Are you risky or conservative? Do you trade options? When do you take profits? Do you sell covered calls?

Only you can develop the right trading plan for yourself   

Here’s very simple example:

On options trades I typically look for 20-30%. Why? Because I’ve been burned by my own greed always wanting more, but Mr. Market doesn’t always like that, so I decided that 20-30% is good for me. 

I also like to sell covered calls as part of my plan. I always set the strike price at a profit I’d be happy with. This way I can collect premiums & if the stock goes down I will still be profitable or at breakeven. I sell the calls on Fridays so that Theta can work for me over the weekend. 

Find your niche: meaning find a strategy that resonates with you & follow it to perfection. I‘be playin off of market fear for the past month with this particular strategy I’ve profited 4/4 times. 

When do you cut your losses? I cut losses at 20%. I’ve decided this is the risk percentage I’m comfortable with. 

Keep in mind this is a basic version of my trading plan. I have found that following my trading plan helps me limit losses. When I don’t follow this plan I have found that I am less profitable. 

Soooooo, what I’m saying is this, if you don’t have a trading plan you should probably develop one. You try it out with a paper trading account if you’d like to see if your plan works for you or if you need to tweak it a bit. At the same time if you’re making money hand over fist then keep doing what you’re doing…. You’ve figured it out, but that’s not the case for most folks. 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/Ltr/do-you-have-a-trading-plan*

### Post #863: 2025-04-19 11:28 — `$SPY, $MSTR`

**Remove emotion from your trades**

I thought I would follow up from yesterday with a post on removing emotion from trading, the downfalls that I’ve struggled with, & how removing emotion has helped me as a trader. 

Firstly we should be honest, we are investing our hard earned money & that makes it personal. When we are personally invested in anything we as humans typically involve emotion. 

I want to be the most successful trader I can possibly be. I want to grow my account fast & have a great tolerance for high risk/high reward. These 2 things led me to trade based on emotion/greed. I wanted to make all the gains on all the trades all the time. Guess what folks, that simply is not going to happen. That’s a fact of trading. 

My greed allowed me to pass up modest gains in hopes of huge gains, only to see those modest gains turn into losses. 

Once I had a trading plan in place it allowed me to remove emotion from the trade because the exit strategy was no longer up to me, it was up to the trading plan. 

My trading plan: Red days are for holding or buying, Green days are for holding or taking profit. When trading options I exit with a 20-30% profit. I only break this rule when trading options on $MSTR & $SPY because of the volatility & potential for astronomical gains. 

Never blindly follow, always do some DD on your own. Take advice from those you’ve gotten to know are trustworthy. Always trade with conviction. 

If I follow my plan I know I’m giving it my all & I can not be upset with the outcome. We are not going to be profitable with every trade, although that is obviously the goal. I do not consider my losses as losses, but rather  as fees paid to become a more profitable trader in the future. As long as I learn from my mistakes then they will be helpful in the future. 

I wish you all good fortune! 🍀 


*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/Lt4/remove-emotion-from-your-trade*

### Post #931: 2025-05-24 13:12 — `$MTPLF`

**I didn’t follow my trading rules 😔**

As many of you know, I have a saying:

Green days are for taking profit. Red days are for buying. Both days are great for holding. 

Well last week I bought more shares of $MTPLF on a Green day. I should’ve waited for a pullback to buy more shares, but I didn’t. The next day the stock was red. I could’ve had a lower cost average & more shares. 

I believe this stock to be a long hold, my thesis & conviction haven’t changed, so I’ll continue buying more shares & averaging down.

My intention with making this post is to remind everyone to have a trading plan & stick to it

Also: checkout the AfterWhores podcast on YouTube if you haven’t already. The vibe is 4 guys ( @ChiefTapaho @Bobdog @daren & me @NewFishBigPond ) hanging out talking stocks & shooting the shit. 

I wish you all good fortune 🍀

### Post #947: 2025-05-31 11:00 — `$BTC`

**888 days**

888 days ago I changed my life in a way I wanted/needed to, & almost lost everything in the process. 

888 days ago I checked myself into rehab for alcohol addiction. While detoxing under medical supervision I came very close to death, literally. So close in fact that the nurse told me the next morning that it was a bit of a scary night to put it lightly. 

That was the day I knew I would do better for myself & my family moving forward. I’ve worked hard to overcome my demons, it’s a daily struggle. 

I’m an addict. If I enjoy something I go full fucking throttle! I need the dopamine hit. 

After I got healthy I was looking for a new hobby. I watched the movie “Dumb Money” & told my wife I thought if they could do it then so could I. That was in late summer ‘24. Since finding AfterHour & learning about the stock market I’ve completed a $10-25K challenge & a $25-50K challenge. Next stop, $100K. I have full confidence I’ll get there by EOY if not sooner. 

If you’ve made it this far then you might be wondering why the hell didn’t I post this on Reddit instead 😂 

Well folks, I’ve traded the bottle for the charts. Last night, Friday 10:30pm, a lot of ppl were out living it up or hanging with friends. What was I doing? I was watching the $BTC  charts, predicted a setup for an inverse H&S, then watched it play out 30-45 minutes after identifying it. That was a great feeling! It also gave me my buy entry to accumulate more $BTC 

I made this post to show that real change & growth is possible with effort. Put in the work. Be the best you can be. Push your limits. Impress yourself!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/PaE/888-days*

**Are you buying $BTC on Robinhood?**

Would you be surprised to hear Robinhood charges you a .7% fee when you buy $BTC? If you have $COIN premium then you simply pay your monthly fee & then pay the actual price of $BTC when you buy. 

Something to keep in mind while you buy the dips

Erebor is not yet open to the public—it’s still in the process of launching.

⸻

🏛️ Where Erebor Stands Now
	•	The bank has filed for a U.S. national bank charter (June 11, 2025), seeking approval from the Office of the Comptroller of the Currency, FDIC, and Federal Reserve  ￼.
	•	The timeline to actual operation may be several months away, as regulators typically provide a conditional charter first, followed by deposit insurance and full operational approval  ￼.

⸻

🚫 Why You Can’t Open an Account Yet
	•	Erebor remains a digital startup, aiming to operate entirely online—but is still awaiting regulatory ✅.
	•	As such, there’s no active app, no banking portal, and no way to deposit funds yet—the infrastructure is still under development.

⸻

📝 When It Becomes Available

Expect a multi-step launch sequence once approvals arrive:
	1.	Conditional charter granted by OCC
	2.	FDIC deposit insurance
	3.	Full operational rollout, including mobile & web access, accepting deposits, stablecoin services, lending, etc.

No public launch date has been set. The founders are targeting tech/crypto startups, but won’t offer accounts until full authorization is in place  ￼.

⸻

✅ Summary
	•	Currently: Application for national charter submitted; waiting on regulator decisions.
	•	Not yet available: No account opening, no services.
	•	Next steps: Watch for OCC/FDIC approvals, then a public launch.

⸻

If you’re curious, I can help you track updates on charter approval or anticipated launch schedule.

### Post #1075: 2025-07-06 10:55 — `$TMC` `[Dd]`

**$TMC DD & why I’m exercising calls**

I bought 100 shares on 7/2 & they’re up 16%. Exercising a call this week in a different account. I see huge upside potential for this company with the passage of the BBB.

Here’s what ChatGPT has to say:

🟢 Bull Case — Why Buy

1. Exposure to critical battery metals

TMC specializes in extracting nickel, cobalt, copper, and manganese from deep‑sea polymetallic nodules—key ingredients for EV batteries and energy storage—positioning it strategically in the green‑transition supply chain  ￼ ￼.

2. Regulatory tailwinds

A recent US executive order allows TMC to sidestep the UN’s International Seabed Authority and pursue commercial mining faster—significantly speeding up project timelines  ￼.

3. Big‑name funding & partnership

In June 2025, Korea Zinc invested ~$85M for a 5% stake—showing strong institutional confidence and strengthening TMC’s financial runway  ￼.

4. Analyst upgrades & sentiment
	•	Wedbush upgraded to “Outperform” with a $11 price target (from $6), barring speculation  ￼.
	•	All 5 covering analysts maintain Buy ratings with an average target ~$7.50  ￼.
	•	TipRanks and TradingView signal strong technical buy patterns and momentum  ￼.

5. High volatility = high upside

The stock recently surged ~22% in two days, hitting highs not seen since 2021, fueled by policy news and strategic deals  ￼.

⸻

🔴 Risks — What Could Go Wrong

1. Pre‑revenue, speculative

TMC has yet to generate any meaningful revenue—they’re years away from commercial output (likely 2027)  ￼. Recent Q1 2025 loss was $0.06/share, with limited liquidity (~$43.8M)  ￼.

2. Environmental & regulatory concerns

Deep‑sea mining is controversial. Governments (EU) and companies (BMW, Google) have called for moratoriums due to potential ecological damage  ￼.

3. Insider selling & dilution

Executives have sold significant shares recently ($1M worth in Q2), and a follow‑on equity offering ($37M) may dilute existing shareholders  ￼.

4. Financial instability

Negative shareholder equity, under $1M in current revenue, and ongoing losses mean additional funding rounds are likely—raising execution and dilution risk  

🧭 So, Should You Buy?

If you’re a high-risk, high-reward investor bullish on deep‑sea mining and EV supply chains—TMC offers unique upside, especially with regulatory catalysts and stakeholder confidence.

However, it’s still highly speculative: no revenue, heavy losses, and environmental/regulatory hurdles remain. Manage position size, track permitting updates, cash burn, and upcoming financing rounds.

⸻

🔭 Key Upcoming Catalysts
	•	Commercial recovery permit under US Deep Seabed Hard Mineral Resources Act
	•	Further analyst updates & price-target revisions (e.g. Wedbush)
	•	Environmental/regulatory backlash or moratorium decisions at the ISA level
	•	Q2/Q3 financial updates on funding, losses, and project milestones

⸻

⚠️ Recommendation

If you choose to invest, treat it as a speculative satellite position (≤ 2–3% of portfolio). Watch regulatory developments and cash runway closely. A trigger could be permit approval or major news, but be ready to act fast in both directions.

### Post #1088: 2025-07-09 21:30

**Why I don’t give up when the market takes my money**

I recently opened a WeBull account with a total investment of ~$600. This is a challenge account…a small cap day trading challenge account. 

As you can see in the pics attached; June wasn’t  good, but July seems to be off to a good start. The overall P&L is off to a decent start, but needs to get much higher

Some ppl may have stopped on June 30th & said, “well this isn’t for me. I’ll stop & just do what I’ve been doing”. That’s not me. I don’t operate like that. 

I gave myself a challenge, and I needed to figure out how to get better. I made some changes in the way I approached the reasons I made my buys. It seems to have worked for the past few days at least. 

Whatever I did doesn’t matter because we’re all different. We have different risk tolerances & reasons  for investing. 

If you’re finding that you need a change in your style then don’t be afraid to switch things up. Think outside the box. Maybe implement something a bit unconventional & see if it works. Remember, it’s only crazy if it doesn’t work

### Post #1094: 2025-07-11 20:45 `[Discuss]`

**Are you beating 97% of traders?**

I asked ChatGPT what the annual ROI is for the average retail trader. Here’s what it said:

The average annual percentage return for the average retail trader is typically low, and in many cases, negative. Here’s a breakdown:

⸻

📊 Key Data from Studies & Brokers:
	1.	Barber and Odean Study (2000s):
	•	Found that individual investors underperform the market by about 1.5% to 3% annually.
	•	Active traders did worse: the most frequent traders had net annual returns of ~11.4%, while the market returned ~17.9%.
	2.	CFA Institute / Dalbar Study:
	•	Over 20-year periods, the average equity mutual fund investor earns about 4% to 5% annually, compared to 8–10% for the S&P 500.
	•	Retail traders typically fare worse than mutual fund investors due to higher trading frequency and poor timing.
	3.	eToro (2021):
	•	The median return of their retail users was less than 3% annually.
	•	A large percentage of accounts (often over 70%) lose money over time.
	4.	Day Traders (Brazil Study – CVM 2020):
	•	Over 97% of active day traders lost money.
	•	Only 0.5% consistently earned returns higher than a bank teller’s salary.

📉 Summary: Average Annual Return
Type of Retail Trader
Avg Annual Return
Passive Index Investor
~7%–10%
Mutual Fund Retail Investor
~4%–5%
Typical Retail Trader
~0% to -3%
Active Day Trader
Negative (most lose money)

⚠️ Why Returns Are Often Low or Negative:
	•	Overtrading & excessive fees
	•	Poor risk management
	•	Emotional decisions (FOMO, panic selling)
	•	Lack of edge or strategy
	•	Chasing hype or meme stocks

📅 IPO Overview
	•	Ticker: HWEP on NASDAQ Capital Market
	•	IPO Date: Expected to begin trading on July 14, 2025  ￼ ￼
	•	Offerings: 4.15 million ADSs at $4.00 each, aiming to raise approximately $16.6 million  ￼
	•	Over-allotment: Underwriters option to issue up to 622.5k additional ADSs (~15%)  ￼

⸻

🚚 Business Model & Growth
	•	Focused on electric light commercial vehicles (eg: ELEMO, ELEMO-K, ELEMO‑L) in Japan and China, manufactured via Cenntro in China  ￼
	•	Notably, ELEMO was the second-ever electric light commercial EV sold in Japan—latest ELEMO-L variant targets both commercial and recreational markets  ￼

⸻

📊 Financial Snapshot (TTM)
	•	Revenue: $1.83 M (up ~27% y/y)
	•	Net Loss: $6.0 M
	•	Market Cap (post-IPO): ~$169 M (assuming no over-allotment)  ￼ ￼
	•	Shares Outstanding: ~42.2 M pre-IPO
	•	EPS: –$0.14 (negative)  ￼

⸻

🛑 Key Risks & Considerations
	1.	No trading history: Market price will be market-driven from day one—no prior levels or benchmarks. ()
	2.	High dilution & immediate dilution risk: Initial book value is ~$0.11 vs IPO price $4.00—implying initial dilution of ~$3.89/share. Further dilution possible via over-allotment or share issuances.  ￼
	3.	Liquidity uncertainties: No guarantee of active post-IPO trading; NASDAQ approval pending.  ￼
	4.	Volatile market reception: Trading price may experience sharp swings due to overall market sentiment, EV sector enthusiasm, analyst coverage, or execution delays. ()

⸻

🎯 Trader Outlook
	•	Upside Potential: If EV investors embrace this niche player and trading volume shows strong demand, there could be rapid price appreciation above the IPO level.
	•	Downside Exposure: Risk of sharp initial drop post-IPO if trading lukewarm, over-supply occurs due to over-allotment, or weak system momentum.
	•	Volatility & Tactics: Expect high volatility; limit orders, tight stop-loss levels, and discipline are critical. Watch early trading flows and underwriter stabilization periods (e.g., overallotment cover).

### Post #1114: 2025-07-18 09:49

**Monthly OPEX 😬**

Be careful today folks…..it’s monthly OPEX.  Specific tickers could be traded a little differently today to ensure share prices close where MMs need them to be for max profit (or as close as they can get). 

If you have shares & the fundamentals or thesis haven’t changed then just hold—you’ll most likely be fine. OPEX can also create buying opportunities if there’s something you want to go long on. My rule is that I do not to buy into long positions on Green Days, just red days. 

I also know traders that sit out trading OPEX

Whatever you do: protect your portfolio & realize your profits

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/TLS/monthly-opex*

📊 Business Overview
	•	Sector: Auto Components—specializing in solid-state rechargeable battery cells to replace liquid electrolytes for enhanced safety, energy density, and compatibility with existing manufacturing  ￼.
	•	Market Cap: Approximately $676–690M  ￼.
	•	IPO: March 24, 2021  ￼.

⸻

🧩 Profitability & Margins
	•	Revenue (TTM): ~$20.2M; Gross Profit: ~$1.5M (≈7.5% margin)  ￼.
	•	Operating Income: –$103.6M; Net Income: –$90.5M; Free Cash Flow: –$75.3M  ￼.
	•	Gross Margin: 7.5%, ranking in the lowest ~7.5% of industry peers  ￼.
	•	Net Margin: –447.8%, Operating Margin: –512.7%  ￼.
	•	Profitability Score: 24/100 — low due to consistent losses  ￼.

⸻

🏥 Financial Health & Solvency
	•	Current Ratio: 23.9; Quick Ratio: ~23.9 — extremely strong liquidity  ￼ ￼.
	•	Debt: Minimal — debt/equity ~0.02; Altman-Z score between ~5.7 and 17.5, indicating low bankruptcy risk  ￼.
	•	Solvency Score: 63/100 — healthy balance sheet  ￼ ￼.

⸻

📈 Growth Metrics
	•	Revenue Growth: TTM +3.2% YoY; ~95% CAGR over 3 years, ~54.6% over 5 years  ￼.
	•	EPS: –$0.51 per share TTM; down 34% YoY, but up 6% quarter-over-quarter  ￼.
	•	Future Outlook:
	•	EPS expected to grow ~8% annually for the next 5 years.
	•	Revenue projected to expand ~28% annually for the next 5 years  ￼ ￼.

⸻

📉 Valuation
	•	P/B: ~1.73–1.74  ￼.
	•	P/S: ~33.8; EV/Sales: ~32.4  ￼.
	•	P/E, EV/EBIT, EV/EBITDA: Not meaningful (due to losses).
	•	Intrinsic Value Estimate: ~$0.17 per share based on combined DCF and comparables—suggests ~96% overvalued at current ~$3.77 price  ￼.

⸻

🧠 Analyst Sentiment & Risks
	•	Price Targets: Average 1‑year target ≈ $2.04 (range: $2.02–$2.10), implying ~44–46% downside  ￼.
	•	MarketScreener View: Labels fundamentals “poor for medium/long‑term,” despite high growth potential and recent earnings surprises  ￼.
	•	Seeking Alpha: Notes first-mover advantage in solid-state batteries and potential for 12–20% annualized returns  ￼.

⚖️ Summary: The Big Picture
Strengths
Risks & Weaknesses
Strong liquidity (Current Ratio ~24)
Significant operating losses (–100M+ annually)
Zero debt
Very low profitability (gross margin ~7.5%, net margin ~–448%)
Rapid revenue growth historically
Valuation stretched (high P/S, P/B ~1.7, price far above intrinsic)
Innovative tech with first-mover edge
Analyst downward targets suggest downside

🚦 Verdict

SLDP is a capital-intensive, high-growth, early-stage player in a cutting-edge battery space. Its strong balance sheet and tech potential are positives, but continued heavy losses and high valuation risks could impact returns. Analyst consensus points to downside, although technological strides may alter the outlook.

### Post #1126: 2025-07-22 11:59 — `$OPEN` `[Discuss]`

**Let’s be $OPEN about this topic**

As of now $OPEN is all over Reddit & Instagram, & there are a lot of buyers stepping in hoping to capitalize on a potential short squeeze. I understand wanting to get rich quick, but if you’re not in this trade already I’m gonna tell you to stay out of it. 

This is when things get a bit dangerous for smaller ports looking for that big win. Shorts have big $$$ invested in their position & they most certainly don’t care about the rent money you just gambled away on Hopium. 

Yes you can probably still get in & make money, but I don’t think it would be a wise decision. Traders must use discipline & not take every trade they want. 

When a stock is sub $4 & the call options chain just got extended to $9, but they don’t even know how to price it yet, that’s a bit scary to me as a trader with a trading plan. 

All I’m saying is know what you’re doing & why you’re getting in: are you trading or are you gambling?

### Post #1142: 2025-08-01 11:57 — `$SPY`

**Reduce the noise—make more profit**

As we all know, the market has been so noisy lately with all the uncertainty mixed with ATHs. Sometimes tuning out the noise is exactly what we need in order to focus on our job of being profitable traders. 

Big shoutout to @trynamakeit & everyone with us trading $SPY in The Kingdom. Yesterday we turned off the news & some of the typical indicators in order to simplify the process, reduce the noise, and focus on the chart. Using this new method of reducing the noise & trusting the trend we were able to catch the entire $SPY drop (puts & calls) from open to close. I entered 3 trades: 2 went over 100% ( I took 80% profit) and 1 went 80% (I took 50% profit).  

The market is doing market things right now, & there’s nothing you can do to change it. Here are your choices: Cash out if you’re scared, sit & weather the storm, or change your approach. I like to learn & adapt.

Wishing you all good fortune 🍀

I remember a statistic I heard that said (numbers could be slightly off here), but the top 30% of earners drive 60-70% of spending. 

I live in a HCOL, vacationed on a cruise line geared towards consumers in a higher income bracket, then spent time in NYC observing the high & medium income earners. 

This economy that I saw & see daily seemed to be doing well. The cash was flowing from wallets to Points of Sale. 

Also, I haven’t looked at the charts (just got home), but I noticed a huge increase in the amount of ppl wearing $NKE the past 2 days vs when I was in NYC 2 months ago

### Post #1194: 2025-09-13 16:33

**The Psychology of Money**

This is a great read when you have time. The book was written to teach us why we invest the way we do. For example, 3 people can buy & sell the same stock at the same price & they will all have different reasons. It’s not just about the money, but our psychology regarding money as well. 

In trading, we know it’s not just what stock to buy, but why is equally as important. So now, we can learn WHY we trade the way we do. 

I wish you all good fortune 🍀 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/Z0h/the-psychology-of-money*

**20k 🤯 Thank you!**

WTAF yall?!?! There are 20,000 AfterHour users that hit the follow button for one reason or another! That’s mind boggling to me. 

Thank you 🙏🏼 

A little background: I grew up lower middle class, didn’t go to college, I’ve seen both extremes of money, I’ve been an addict, & thankfully I got sober 12/25/22

Summer ‘24 I watched the movie “Dumb Money” & thought, if they can do that so can I! So, I joined AfterHour in September of ‘24 (1 year ago) without knowing what a call option was. 

Since then I’ve taken the time to scour every post I can, ask ALL the questions I didn’t understand, learn, & absorb as much as possible about the market. 

Here I am one year later, & I’m pretty sure I’ve found a true passion that I’ll turn into my career. The fact is that 97% of traders fail. I’m still here, I’m still standing, & I’m not fucking leaving. 

To those that have followed me, thank you 🙏🏼Your support really does mean a lot. To the haters, I hope you get something from my content even if it’s just a laugh 😂 

TWENTY FUCKING THOUSAND PEOPLE?!?! I am fucking astonished. Thank you!

To the newer members: Never hesitate to ask questions. I guarantee the person you’re asking once had to ask the same thing. 

Remember, patience pays, & trade the trend

I wish you all good fortune 🍀

### Post #1233: 2025-10-01 10:02

**The US Government is shutdown, OH NO!!**

Here we are, 10/1/26 & the US Government has entered its first day of a TEMPORARY shutdown. Yes you’re going to see some panic from retail traders. They’ll sell today, & cry about it next week. We often see a market overreaction to things. 

The fact is that a Government shutdown doesn’t last forever. In fact, they’re usually relatively short lived. Some of our lives may be impacted temporarily, but it’s exactly what I just said—temporary. 

Soon enough the US Government will be reopened, retail that sold today will pile back in, & stonks go up. 

Keep an eye out for deals on your favorites & buy the dip. I know I will

### Post #1255: 2025-10-09 01:04 — `$BFLY` `[Dd]`

**Thinking about a starter position in $BFLY**

Keep in mind this trade is for high risk/high reward traders. The new CEO took a salary of $1 & stock options 👀 Here’s what ChatGPT had to say:

🏥 Butterfly Network, Inc. (Ticker: BFLY)
	•	Butterfly develops, manufactures, and markets ultrasound imaging solutions.  ￼
	•	Their flagship product line includes handheld, whole-body ultrasound probes (e.g. Butterfly iQ+, iQ3) that connect to smartphones, tablets, or compatible systems.  ￼
	•	They use a technology called Ultrasound-on-Chip (a semiconductor-based approach) to shrink the hardware complexity of traditional ultrasound machines.  ￼
	•	In addition, they provide software, cloud services, workflow integration, educational tools, and support for large deployments in healthcare systems.  ￼ •	Their mission is to “democratize medical imaging” — make ultrasound more accessible, affordable, and usable in more settings (not just big hospitals) around the world.  ￼

Here’s a summary of what’s going in Butterfly Network’s favor — and against it:

✅ Bullish / Strengths
	•	Revenue growth: Their trailing-12-month revenue is about $87.52 million, up significantly year over year.  ￼
	•	Top-line momentum: In Q4 2024, they reported revenue of $22.4M (up ~35% YoY) and improved gross margins (61.4%) vs earlier periods.  ￼
	•	Cash infusion: They raised ~$81.7 million in a public offering in early 2025, strengthening their cash position.  ￼
	•	Strategic initiatives: The company is trying to expand into areas like “HomeCare,” AI, enterprise adoption, and partnerships (e.g. AI development, software) to diversify beyond just selling ultrasound hardware.  

Analyst optimism: Some price targets are relatively bullish. For instance, Zacks lists an average target of ~$4.17.  ￼
	•	Support & technical signals: Some technical analysts point to support levels and bullish signals from moving averages, suggesting there might be some upside in the near term.  ￼

⚠️ Risks / Weaknesses
	•	Ongoing losses: The company is not yet profitable. In recent quarters, they’ve posted negative net income.  ￼
	•	High operating expenses / burn: Their operating expenses are substantial relative to revenue, and they are burning cash.  ￼
	•	Dilution risk: Because they’re not profitable, they may need to issue more shares or raise capital, which can dilute existing shareholders.  ￼
	•	Competitive pressure & market risk: The hand-held ultrasound / medical imaging space is quite competitive. Some analyses suggest Butterfly’s devices may be underperforming relative to competitors in certain metrics.  ￼
	•	Volatility & speculative nature: The stock is volatile and likely to be sensitive to news, regulatory changes, technological shifts, and execution risks.  ￼
	•	Mixed analyst consensus: Some firms rate it “Hold” rather than “Buy.” For example, MarketBeat’s consensus is “Hold,” with average price targets around $3.00.  ￼


*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/4s0/thinking-about-a-starter-posit*

Others, like me, saw a buying opportunity. So I basically “shopped till I dropped”. It was like Black Friday @ Best Buy—discounts everywhere.

### Post #1276: 2025-10-12 12:25 `[Discuss]`

**Fear & Extreme Fear—Do not panic sell everything!**

Friday was a bit of a doozy with Tariff man doing his thing. I’m REALLY not concerned about this as we should be used to it by now. 

Stock Market Fear/Greed Index: Fear
Crypto Fear/Greed Index: Extreme Fear

Did I panic sell my holdings? Nope. Did I buy more, yep. 

I should’ve waited a bit to buy, but to me a discount is a discount. Time in the market beats timing the market. 

To the newer traders: You probably saw a large percentage drop in your portfolio, & that can be scary. Trust me I know, my port sees 5-8% swings some days (crypto proxies can be volatile). However, these are the times when traders gain experience & portfolios can be setup very nicely for the market recovery. 

Only you know what’s best for your portfolio. As for me, the market is not burning to the ground, we will recover as we always do, & I’ll continue to buy the dips. 

“Be greedy when others are fearful, and fearful when others are greedy”—some rich dude from Omaha who might know a thing or two about the market.

### Post #1311: 2025-10-27 00:55 `[Discuss]`

**How do you invest in yourself?**

Obviously we’re all here for the same reason, we’ve put money into an account & we’re here to make money in the market. 

I’m wondering how do you invest in yourself? 

I used to go out & spend a lot of money “doing nothing”  or buying new fancy items I don’t really need for my daily life. I spent a lot of time just out & about, not doing anything to further myself. 

 Instead I now put most of my extra time & money in the market. I look for deals. I buy the fear. I invest in my conviction. I constantly try to learn more & more every single day. 

On the weekends I’m thinking about stocks & looking for trade ideas for the coming week. I pay attention to the news bc we all know that a social media post or a press conference can & does move markets. I pay attention to futures at night/morning to get an idea of where the market is going. I research. I do 2 podcasts. 

During the week I start my day at 4 am EST with premarket & trade through regular session close. I pay attention to aftermarket (and sometimes trade it) so I have an idea of what to watch the following premarket. Then I trade futures during Asia premarket & regular session. 

I invest in myself by using my time & money to better myself & my future. 

So tell me, what are you doing to invest in yourself?




*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/7yH/how-do-you-invest-in-yourself*

### Post #1317: 2025-10-28 09:10

**Don’t let someone talk you out of your conviction**

Have you ever been in a trade that you had a great deal of conviction in only to have that conviction swayed by someone else? I’m sure we’ve all done this at one point & if you haven’t, well aren’t you special 😂 

The fact is, when you enter a trade you entered for a reason. YOU saw something with potential. That is YOUR trade. Treat it as such. Take ownership of the trade. Otherwise, you’ll be left sitting there saying, “why did I realize that stupid loss when I could’ve actually made great profit??”  Trust yourself, it’s your portfolio 

Good luck out there folks 🍀

### Post #1331: 2025-11-15 16:19

**I didn’t buy enough!**

Every single one of us has thought this at some point in our trading career. If you haven’t then either you just started, you’re a fucking genius & should be working on Wall St, or you’re lying. 

It comes down to a few things:

Were you trading & then realized you should’ve been investing in that company?

Was it a YOLO or Copy Trade?

Did you do your own DD? Any at all?

There’s nothing wrong with saying no to any of these questions, but please know that you need to put in a little more effort if you want to make money in the Market. This is a business not a game & we should all treat it as such. If you didn’t have enough money then don’t worry about it bc you bought what you could afford at the time. Other trades will come around

If you play a YOLO there’s no problems—you do you. I play them too. However, that’s a play & not a trade. 

Lastly I’ll leave you with this:

You’re only competing against yourself. Compounded gains are how you keep a healthy portfolio 

Wishing you all good fortune 🍀 


*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/bajo/i-didnt-buy-enough*

**Did your portfolio have a hedge?**

I hedge my portfolio a few different ways: LETFs, VIX, SOXS, & Metals. 

The idea that a VIX option would go to zero is the best case scenario, but if that doesn’t happen you want your port protected from downside exposure. Hedges are a necessary form of risk management if you want to survive as a trader. (The VIX is up almost 22% in premarket)

The importance of paying attention to the Macro environment is something I can not stress enough. This isn’t about Red or Blue it’s about Green. Keep your eyes & ears open. Use your critical thinking skills. Stay ahead of the curve. 

Concerning our new self-imposed conflict in the Middle East: If you didn’t foresee the attack on Iran happening this past weekend then YOU are who this post is meant for. 

Be safe out there & I wish you all good fortune 🍀 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/biSP/did-your-portfolio-have-a-hedg*

### Post #1483: 2026-03-27 11:49

**30,000 followers 🤯**

When I watched the movie “Dumb Money” in the summer of ‘24, I thought it might be interesting to learn about the world of trading. I had no idea about how to make money in the market other than to “let my guy handle it for me”. 

In September of ‘24 I found AfterHour. I was the New Fish in the Big Pond of trading, but after a while the OGs took the time to answer my “dumb questions”. I asked *all* the questions. I followed ppl who produced content that I liked & spoke to me.  I made some great gains, & had some very bad losses. Every trade is a learning opportunity. 

To those of you who may just be starting in the world of trading, this is a tough market to navigate for most. At the same time it provides excellent learning opportunities. 

My advice: ask the questions, have a trading plan with a Stop loss & Take profit, followers creators that produce content that you enjoy!

Again, I’m not sure how the *fuck* this happened,  but thank you to the +30,000 people that decided to follow me 🙏 


*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/bkIw/30000-followers*

### Post #1524: 2026-04-18 20:03 — `$DVLT` `[Dd]`

**ChatGPT Bull & Bear case arguments for $DVLT**

Yesterday I bought up DataVault AI $DVLT. As I said, DD isn’t my strong suit so I asked Chat to give us something. Never trust AI without verifying on your own

Here’s a clean, trader-focused bull vs. bear breakdown of $DVLT (Datavault AI) based on the most current fundamentals + catalysts.

⸻

🟢 Bull Case (Why DVLT could rip)

1. Hyper-growth + first profitability inflection

* Revenue exploded +1,300% YoY to ~$39M (2025)  
* First profitable quarter (Q4 2025) → huge psychological shift  
* Gross margins ~78%+ → software-like economics  

👉 This is classic “pre-profit → profit” re-rating setup (these can go parabolic in small caps)

⸻

2. Massive forward guidance (if real)

* Targeting $200M revenue in 2026 (~400% growth)  

👉 If they even hit HALF of that, valuation looks cheap relative to AI peers

⸻

3. AI + infrastructure narrative (hot sector)

* Positioned in:
    * AI data monetization
    * HPC / GPU infrastructure
    * Tokenization / Web3 rails  
* Rolling out:
    * 48,000 GPU edge network
    * Target: 1,000 sites across 100+ cities  

👉 That gives it a NVDA-adjacent narrative tailwind (even if smaller scale)

⸻

4. Balance sheet surprisingly clean

* ~$116M working capital, no long-term debt  

👉 For a microcap, this is a BIG deal (reduces dilution risk short-term)

⸻

5. Analyst upside + retail attention

* Price targets around $3–$4 (~300%+ upside)  
* High volume, trending ticker activity  

👉 This is exactly the type of stock that can squeeze hard on momentum

⸻

🔴 Bear Case (Why this could get smoked)

1. Massive dilution + share structure risk

* Shares outstanding exploded +1000%+ YoY  

👉 This is a HUGE red flag

* Any future funding = more dilution
* Hard to sustain price appreciation long-term

⸻

2. Still deeply unprofitable overall

* Full-year net income: - ~$79M  
* Negative free cash flow

👉 One profitable quarter ≠ sustainable profitability

⸻

3. Execution risk on aggressive projections

* Going from $39M → $200M in one year is extreme
* Requires:
    * flawless execution
    * successful acquisitions
    * actual demand for their products

👉 Most companies miss projections like this

⸻

4. Business model complexity (and hype risk)

They’re doing:

* AI
* Data monetization
* Tokenization
* HPC infrastructure
* Audio tech (!!)

👉 That’s a lot of narratives packed into one company

* Can be innovation… or lack of focus

⸻

5. Stock behavior = classic small-cap volatility

* Down ~16% over 1 year despite hype  
* Range: $0.25 → $4.10  

👉 This screams:

* dilution cycles
* hype spikes → fade

⸻

6. Low institutional coverage

* Only 1 analyst covering it  

👉 Translation:

* Not widely validated
* Easier to manipulate / overhype

⸻

⚖️ My Real Take (Trader Lens)

This is NOT a traditional investment — it’s a high-beta momentum + story stock.

🧠 What matters most:

* Can they convert hype → real revenue in 2026?
* Does dilution slow down?
* Does the GPU / AI narrative catch retail flow?

⸻

🎯 How I’d Frame It (Based on your trading style)

You’re a momentum/options trader — so think:

🔥 Bull scenario:

* Breaks key resistance + volume
* AI narrative heats up
* → Fast 50–150% moves possible

❄️ Bear scenario:

* Dilution / offering hits
* Revenue misses / hype fades
* → -30% to -60% rug pulls

⸻

Bottom Line

* Bull case: Early-stage AI infra play with insane growth + narrative = explosive upside
* Bear case: Dilution-heavy microcap with aggressive projections = classic trap setup

### Post #1537: 2026-04-27 21:00

**Quantum Sensing—what the fuck is it & why is it so important??**

I just heard about Quantum Sensing roughly 10 minutes ago so forgive me for not being able to properly explain it yet. What I will say is that this tech will absolutely change the future of medical technology & other sectors. I’m sure someone smarter than me like @MrShorty is already invested or figuring out where to put his money.  Perhaps this shameless attempt to ask @I_killed_the_kraken to do a DD will be successful (DD isn’t my strong suit—know your strengths & weaknesses)

Here’s what ChatGPT had to say when asked to ELI5 Quantum Sensing:

The Simple Idea

Imagine you have a super-powered ruler.

* A normal ruler → can measure inches
* A quantum ruler → can measure something so tiny… it’s like detecting a single grain of sand moving on Earth from space

That’s basically quantum sensing.

👉 It’s a way to measure things WAY more precisely than normal sensors

⸻

🧠 Even Simpler Analogy

Think of it like this:

* Normal sensors = regular microphone
* Quantum sensors = a microphone so sensitive it can hear a pin drop… on Mars

That’s not an exaggeration — scientists literally compare it to detecting insanely tiny signals like that  

⸻

⚛️ What makes it “quantum”?

Instead of using normal physics, it uses tiny particles (atoms, electrons) as the measuring tool.

These tiny things behave in weird ways:

* They act like tiny magnets
* They can exist in super precise energy states
* They react to very small changes in the environment

👉 Because of that, they can detect things normal sensors can’t  

⸻

🎯 What can it actually measure?

Quantum sensors can detect insanely small changes in things like:

* Gravity
* Time
* Magnetic fields
* Movement
* Light

Way more precisely than traditional tech  

⸻

🚀 Real-world examples (this is the important part)

These already exist:

* Atomic clocks → keep time so precise they barely drift in millions of years
* MRI machines → use quantum effects to scan your body
* Navigation without GPS → works even if GPS is jammed

⸻

🔥 Why this actually matters (your edge)

This isn’t sci-fi like quantum computing — it’s closer to real money:

* Military → GPS-denied navigation
* Oil/mining → detect underground resources
* Trading angle → defense + infrastructure contracts = revenue sooner

👉 Quantum sensing is expected to hit real-world adoption faster than quantum computing

⸻

🧠 One-line takeaway

Quantum sensing = using atoms as ultra-precise measuring tools to detect things that were previously invisible.

⸻

If you want, I can break it down even further into:

* Which use cases will make money first
* Or which stocks could rip on quantum sensing headlines (there’s a clear setup forming)

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/bm5J/quantum-sensingwhat-the-fuck-i*

Seriously, I’m not sure why ppl are on this app begging for tips. Tips here have absolutely no monetary value to anyone except AfterHour who’s selling them to the user base. The person receiving the tip gets absolutely ZERO monetary compensation for your tip. Instead, they’re entered into a raffle (along with everyone else that received a tip that week). This raffle nets the winner with $100–that’s it. This tip system is designed to liberate money from the user base in order to a create a “dick swinging” contest among content creators. So as you can see, the tips don’t mean shit. 

Don’t tip me please. Save them for the ppl literally begging for tips 🤣 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/bnrf/why-do-yall-care-about-these-t*

### Post #1694: 2026-08-01 17:23

**There’s been a lot of talk about different trading styles this weekend. Let’s try to understand each other a little bit**

If you haven’t read it yet, I highly suggest you read/listen to The Psychology of Money. This was the first book I read when I got into the world of trading. 

The book discusses the reasons *why* we trade the way we do. Many of you think it’s due to your college education or life experience after college. Many of you didn’t go to college, & you have a TON of life experience. 

The thing is, the Author says those events do not typically inspire the way we trade, & that much of it is due to our upbringing. Some folks are extremely conservative, some middle of the road, & some are looking to break into Fort Knox with every trade using the most amount of leverage possible. 

I honestly don’t give a fuck why you trade the way you do. We’ve all experienced different upbringings. Just know that none of us are the same, & yet we are all alike. 

Read the book. Stay true to yourself. Fuckers get salty when they’re losing & proven wrong

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/btu0/theres-been-a-lot-of-talk-abou*

### Post #1749: 2026-08-15 13:07 — `$MGRX` `[Dd]`

**Here’s what Claude says about $MGRX**

I had Claude ELI5 both a Bull & Bear case. Obviously this is merely a guide for you to do your own research:

MGRX used to be a small telehealth company selling ED pills online. Now it’s basically an empty shell that a nuclear reactor startup (Nuclea Energy) is using as a backdoor to get onto Nasdaq — like a small company “putting on a costume” of a big new business. The stock’s wild swings right now are people betting on whether that nuclear deal actually closes.

Where it stands (Aug 13-14, 2026): Shares ~$0.53-0.72, market cap roughly $9-19M depending on the day — this thing moves 20-50%+ in a session regularly. Volume has been running 15-40x normal on the nuclear headlines.

Bull Case

	•	The core trade isn’t Mango anymore — it’s Nuclea. On July 29, 2026, Mango signed a definitive deal where Nuclea Energy (a nuclear microreactor developer) reverse-merges into it. Post-close, Nuclea holders would own roughly 96% of the outstanding equity of Mangoceuticals on a fully diluted ￼ basis — this is essentially Nuclea buying a Nasdaq ticker.
	•	Real-sounding partnerships stacking up fast: since the deal was announced, Nuclea has signed an MOU with Utah’s Office of Energy Development to explore siting a nuclear test reactor at the Utah San Rafael Energy Lab ￼, a deal to acquire Moltex Energy’s advanced nuclear technology assets ￼, and an MOU with a mining company to explore deploying Nuclea’s modular reactor technology as behind-the-meter power for high-density computing and Bitcoin mining operations ￼.
	•	Thematic tailwind: it’s riding the AI/data-center power-demand narrative — small-cap nuclear plays have been magnets for speculative flow all year.
	•	Nasdaq gave it room to survive: an extension to Feb 1, 2027 to fix the listing deficiency, so it won’t get delisted imminently.

Bear Case

	•	It’s not a done deal. The transaction still needs regulatory, competition, and stockholder greenlights—including those from Nasdaq, Investment Canada Act, and Hart-Scott-Rodino ￼. Any of those can slip or fail.
	•	Massive built-in dilution. Existing MGRX holders get diluted down to ~4% of the company. You’re not really buying “Mango upside” — you’re buying a sliver of a pre-revenue nuclear concept company, and a concurrent PIPE financing of at least $15,000,000 ￼ is required just to fund the first closing, meaning more shares get printed.
	•	Nasdaq compliance is precarious. The company is below the $1 minimum bid requirement and may need a reverse split to cure it — reverse splits in microcaps are usually bearish signals and often precede further share-price erosion.
	•	The “reactor” is vaporware-stage. Nuclea’s Morpheus reactor is remains in the conceptual design stage ￼, on an 18-month roadmap to even reach regulatory/commercial readiness — years from any actual revenue or working hardware.
	•	History of legal/operational messiness: the company has an active lawsuit against a former tech vendor and a track record of filing delays, which adds execution risk on top of the merger risk.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/bums/heres-what-claude-says-about-d*

### Post #1750: 2026-08-15 13:18 — `$QUCY`

**Bull & Bear case for $QUCY**

AfterHour tags this company incorrectly. The company  being talked about here is Quantum Computing N.V.

I had Claude ELI5 both a Bull & Bear case for $QUCY. You can use this as a guide to do your own research if this stock interests you

$QUCY  used to be a shell company called Mynz that got taken over earlier this year by David Lazar (a well-known microcap “shell flipper”) and rebranded as a drone/defense/quantum-antenna company. It just raised real cash, bought a factory, and is chasing military contracts — but it’s tiny, loses a lot of money, and just got accused publicly of misleading investors.

Where it stands (Aug 13, 2026): ~$1.30-1.55/share, market cap roughly $19-38M (varies a lot by source/day), 52-week range $0.30-$4.93 — extremely volatile.

Bull Case:

•	Fresh, clean balance sheet. In May 2026 the company brought in over $15,000,000 in gross proceeds from warrant holders fully exercising outstanding warrants, ￼ and it’s marketed itself as debt-free with the at-the-market dilution facility terminated in June — a rare “no near-term forced dilution” story for a microcap.
	•	Real assets, not just PowerPoint. It closed on a Bridgeport facility acquisition in July 2026 ￼ — an actual owned manufacturing plant — and holds a definitive exclusive quantum-antenna license (Project LightShift) ￼ signed in June.
	•	Government pipeline traction: it submitted a confidential white paper to a live US Army down-select ￼ in July, and per Yahoo Finance it’s been securing $2.1 million in new orders, ￼ positioning around the “quantum + AI + drone defense” theme that’s popular with investors right now (counter-UAS, border security, autonomous systems).
	•	Analyst enthusiasm: it picked up a $9 price target and new ‘Buy’ rating from a Litchfield Hills analyst ￼ on August 11 — a huge implied upside from the current ~$1.30-1.50 (take small-cap sell-side price targets with a big grain of salt, but it shows someone’s willing to publish a bullish thesis).
	•	Product launches: it unveiled the PHANTOM-950, a long-range autonomous unmanned aerial system aimed at government and defense customers ￼ with plans for domestic manufacturing.

Bear Case:

This was a shell-company reverse takeover, and by a name worth knowing. In February–April 2026, David Lazar bought control via a purchase agreement, and the Second Closing Shares represented in excess of 90% of issued and outstanding Ordinary Shares on a fully diluted basis, ￼ making him the controlling shareholder. Lazar has a long track record of flipping distressed shells into “hot theme” tickers (crypto, AI, defense, etc.) — that pattern alone should raise your guard on durability of the story.
	•	Brutal dilution already happened. Shareholders have been substantially diluted in the past year with a 312% increase in shares outstanding, ￼ and shares outstanding are already up from ~22.8M in May to ~27.6M by July.
	•	The financials are ugly. FY2025: $537K in revenue, operating income of -$16.1M (-3005% operating margin), net loss of -$16.2M, diluted EPS of -$2.70, ￼ with operating cash flow of -$11.0M. ￼ Revenue barely exists relative to the cash burn.
	•	Live market-manipulation dispute cuts both ways. The company is suing a short-seller research firm (White Diamond) and Stocktwits for a report published June 29, 2026 that the company alleges caused a roughly 13.7% decline in QUCY shares between June 26 and June 30. ￼ That means there’s a published bearish report out there challenging the business/CEO claims — worth reading before sizing a position, since the lawsuit itself doesn’t resolve whether the criticisms were accurate.
	•	Rising short interest: short interest has increased by 492.4% since March 2026, ￼ now sitting around 13.3% of the float — meaningful bearish positioning building underneath the bullish press releases.
	•	Everything is “definitive agreement” and “MOU” stage, not revenue. The Army contract is a white paper into a “down-select,” not an award; the quantum-antenna license and drone products are early-stage.

Net: Classic controlled-shell-into-hot-theme microcap — real cash on the balance sheet and real PR momentum, but also real dilution history, a controlling shareholder with a shell-flipping reputation, and an active credibility fight with a short-seller. This is a story stock where the next incremental catalyst (Army down-select result, next quarterly cash burn update, any new share issuance despite “ATM terminated”) will move it fast in either direction.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/NewFishBigPond/bumt/bull-and-bear-case-for-dollarq*

If you’re trading tomorrow I wish you good luck. If you’re seeing red in positions you believe in (the only positions that should be in your port) then just remember that an unrealized loss is exactly that—unrealized. Be smart, Be cautious, & Size appropriately
